# Query traces with duration > 1 second
curl "http://localhost:9411/api/v2/traces?minDuration=1000000"
Production Recommendations
Component	Production Configuration
Elasticsearch	Use cluster (3+ nodes), increase heap size, use SSDs
Logstash	Increase queue size, use persistent queues, multiple pipelines
Kibana	Enable security, use reverse proxy, configure SAML/LDAP
Zipkin	Use Elasticsearch backend, sample at 1-10% in production
Sampling	Reduce Sleuth sampler to 0.1 (10%) in production
Log Rotation	Implement log rotation and retention policies (30 days)
This complete setup gives you:

Centralized logging across all microservices

Distributed tracing for request flow visualization

Correlation IDs for end-to-end tracking

Structured JSON logs for easy parsing

Real-time dashboards in Kibana

Service dependency graphs in Zipkin

How to provide reporting from distributed microservices data ?
Providing reporting from distributed microservices is challenging because data is scattered across multiple databases, each owned by different services. You cannot simply run a SQL JOIN across services. This requires a fundamentally different approach than monolithic reporting.

The Core Problem
text
Monolith Reporting                    Distributed Reporting
┌─────────────────────┐              ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     │              │  Order Service──┐                    │
│  Single Database    │              │  (PostgreSQL)   │                    │
│                     │              │                 │                    │
│  SELECT * FROM      │              │  Payment Service─┼─┐                  │
│  orders o           │              │  (MySQL)        │ │                  │
│  JOIN customers c   │    VS        │                 │ │                  │
│  JOIN payments p    │              │  Shipping Service┼─┼─┐                │
│  WHERE ...          │              │  (PostgreSQL)   │ │ │                │
│                     │              │                 │ │ │                │
└─────────────────────┘              │  Inventory Svc──┘ │ │                │
                                     │  (MongoDB)        │ │                │
                                     │                   │ │                │
                                     │  Customer Svc──────┘ │                │
                                     │  (PostgreSQL)        │                │
                                     │                      │                │
                                     │  Report Service───────┘                │
                                     └─────────────────────────────────────┘
Reporting Architecture Patterns






























Pattern 1: API Composition (Simple Reports)
Best for: Small reports, low data volume, real-time requirements

java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/reports")
@Slf4j
public class ReportCompositionController {
    
    @Autowired
    private OrderServiceClient orderClient;
    
    @Autowired
    private CustomerServiceClient customerClient;
    
    @Autowired
    private PaymentServiceClient paymentClient;
    
    @GetMapping("/order-summary/{customerId}")
    public CompletableFuture<CustomerReport> getCustomerReport(@PathVariable Long customerId) {
        
        // Parallel calls to multiple services
        CompletableFuture<CustomerInfo> customerFuture = 
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> customerClient.getCustomer(customerId));
        
        CompletableFuture<List<OrderSummary>> ordersFuture = 
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> orderClient.getCustomerOrders(customerId));
        
        CompletableFuture<PaymentHistory> paymentsFuture = 
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> paymentClient.getCustomerPayments(customerId));
        
        // Combine results
        return CompletableFuture.allOf(customerFuture, ordersFuture, paymentsFuture)
            .thenApply(v -> {
                CustomerReport report = new CustomerReport();
                report.setCustomer(customerFuture.join());
                report.setOrders(ordersFuture.join());
                report.setPayments(paymentsFuture.join());
                report.setTotalOrders(report.getOrders().size());
                report.setTotalSpent(calculateTotalSpent(report.getPayments()));
                report.setGeneratedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
                return report;
            });
    }
}
Pros: Simple, real-time, no extra infrastructure
Cons: Slow for large datasets, N+1 problem, service dependency

Pattern 2: CQRS with Reporting Database (Recommended)
Best for: Most reporting scenarios, good balance of complexity and performance

Architecture
text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                           MICROSERVICES                                      │
├──────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┬────────────────────────────────┤
│ Order Service│Customer Svc  │Payment Svc   │Inventory Svc                   │
│   (Write)    │   (Write)    │   (Write)    │   (Write)                      │
└──────┬───────┴──────┬───────┴──────┬───────┴───────┬────────────────────────┘
       │              │              │               │
       │ (Events)     │ (Events)     │ (Events)      │ (Events)
       ▼              ▼              ▼               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              KAFKA                                           │
│  Topics: order.events, customer.events, payment.events, inventory.events   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │
                                    ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                      REPORTING SERVICE                                       │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │
│  │                    Event Consumers                                   │    │
│  │  - OrderEventHandler                                                │    │
│  │  - CustomerEventHandler                                             │    │
│  │  - PaymentEventHandler                                              │    │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
│                                    │                                         │
│                                    ▼                                         │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │
│  │                REPORTING DATABASE (Denormalized)                     │    │
│  │  - order_summary_view (denormalized)                                │    │
│  │  - customer_360_view                                                │    │
│  │  - daily_sales_aggregates                                           │    │
│  │  - materialized views                                               │    │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │
                                    ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         REPORT API                                          │
│  - REST endpoints for reporting                                            │
│  - Pagination, filtering, aggregation                                      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
Implementation
Step 1: Reporting Database Schema (PostgreSQL)
sql
-- Denormalized order summary table (for fast queries)
CREATE TABLE order_summary_report (
    order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    order_number VARCHAR(50),
    order_status VARCHAR(20),
    order_date TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Customer denormalized fields
    customer_id UUID,
    customer_name VARCHAR(100),
    customer_email VARCHAR(100),
    customer_tier VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Payment denormalized fields
    payment_id UUID,
    payment_status VARCHAR(20),
    payment_amount DECIMAL(10,2),
    payment_method VARCHAR(50),
    payment_date TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Shipping denormalized fields
    shipping_status VARCHAR(20),
    shipping_carrier VARCHAR(50),
    tracking_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Product summary (as JSON for flexibility)
    products JSONB,
    total_quantity INT,
    total_amount DECIMAL(10,2),
    
    -- Metadata
    last_updated TIMESTAMP,
    version INT DEFAULT 1
);

-- Customer 360 report table
CREATE TABLE customer_360_report (
    customer_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    customer_name VARCHAR(100),
    customer_email VARCHAR(100),
    customer_phone VARCHAR(20),
    customer_tier VARCHAR(20),
    registration_date DATE,
    total_orders INT DEFAULT 0,
    total_spent DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    average_order_value DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    last_order_date TIMESTAMP,
    last_order_amount DECIMAL(10,2),
    preferred_payment_method VARCHAR(50),
    preferred_shipping_address JSONB,
    
    -- Aggregates by period
    orders_last_30_days INT DEFAULT 0,
    spent_last_30_days DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    orders_last_90_days INT DEFAULT 0,
    spent_last_90_days DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    last_updated TIMESTAMP
);

-- Daily sales aggregate (precomputed for dashboard)
CREATE TABLE daily_sales_aggregate (
    sale_date DATE,
    total_orders INT DEFAULT 0,
    total_revenue DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
    average_order_value DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,
    unique_customers INT DEFAULT 0,
    top_product_category VARCHAR(50),
    PRIMARY KEY (sale_date)
);

-- Indexes for reporting queries
CREATE INDEX idx_order_summary_customer ON order_summary_report(customer_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_order_summary_date ON order_summary_report(order_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_order_summary_status ON order_summary_report(order_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_customer_360_tier ON customer_360_report(customer_tier);
CREATE INDEX idx_customer_360_total_spent ON customer_360_report(total_spent DESC);
Step 2: Event Handlers for Reporting Service
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class OrderEventHandler {
    
    @Autowired
    private OrderSummaryReportRepository reportRepository;
    
    @Autowired
    private Customer360Repository customer360Repository;
    
    @Autowired
    private DailySalesAggregateRepository dailySalesRepository;
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order.events", groupId = "reporting-group")
    public void handleOrderEvent(ConsumerRecord<String, OrderEvent> record) {
        OrderEvent event = record.value();
        
        switch (event.getEventType()) {
            case "ORDER_CREATED":
                handleOrderCreated(event);
                break;
            case "ORDER_PAID":
                handleOrderPaid(event);
                break;
            case "ORDER_SHIPPED":
                handleOrderShipped(event);
                break;
            case "ORDER_COMPLETED":
                handleOrderCompleted(event);
                break;
            case "ORDER_CANCELLED":
                handleOrderCancelled(event);
                break;
        }
    }
    
    @Transactional
    public void handleOrderCreated(OrderEvent event) {
        log.info("Processing ORDER_CREATED event: {}", event.getOrderId());
        
        // Create denormalized order summary
        OrderSummaryReport report = new OrderSummaryReport();
        report.setOrderId(event.getOrderId());
        report.setOrderNumber(event.getOrderNumber());
        report.setOrderStatus("CREATED");
        report.setOrderDate(event.getOrderDate());
        report.setCustomerId(event.getCustomerId());
        report.setTotalAmount(event.getTotalAmount());
        report.setProducts(event.getProducts());
        report.setTotalQuantity(event.getTotalQuantity());
        report.setLastUpdated(LocalDateTime.now());
        
        reportRepository.save(report);
        
        // Update customer 360 - increment order count
        customer360Repository.findById(event.getCustomerId()).ifPresentOrElse(
            customer -> {
                customer.setTotalOrders(customer.getTotalOrders() + 1);
                customer.setTotalSpent(customer.getTotalSpent() + event.getTotalAmount());
                customer.setAverageOrderValue(
                    customer.getTotalSpent() / customer.getTotalOrders()
                );
                customer360Repository.save(customer);
            },
            () -> {
                // Create new customer 360 record (will be updated by customer event later)
                Customer360Report newCustomer = new Customer360Report();
                newCustomer.setCustomerId(event.getCustomerId());
                newCustomer.setTotalOrders(1);
                newCustomer.setTotalSpent(event.getTotalAmount());
                newCustomer.setAverageOrderValue(event.getTotalAmount());
                customer360Repository.save(newCustomer);
            }
        );
    }
    
    @Transactional
    public void handleOrderPaid(OrderEvent event) {
        reportRepository.findById(event.getOrderId()).ifPresent(report -> {
            report.setOrderStatus("PAID");
            report.setPaymentId(event.getPaymentId());
            report.setPaymentStatus("COMPLETED");
            report.setPaymentAmount(event.getPaymentAmount());
            report.setPaymentMethod(event.getPaymentMethod());
            report.setPaymentDate(event.getPaymentDate());
            report.setLastUpdated(LocalDateTime.now());
            reportRepository.save(report);
        });
    }
    
    @Transactional
    public void handleOrderCompleted(OrderEvent event) {
        // Update order summary
        reportRepository.findById(event.getOrderId()).ifPresent(report -> {
            report.setOrderStatus("COMPLETED");
            report.setLastUpdated(LocalDateTime.now());
            reportRepository.save(report);
        });
        
        // Update daily sales aggregate
        LocalDate saleDate = event.getOrderDate().toLocalDate();
        DailySalesAggregate daily = dailySalesRepository.findById(saleDate)
            .orElse(new DailySalesAggregate(saleDate));
        
        daily.setTotalOrders(daily.getTotalOrders() + 1);
        daily.setTotalRevenue(daily.getTotalRevenue() + event.getTotalAmount());
        daily.setAverageOrderValue(daily.getTotalRevenue() / daily.getTotalOrders());
        
        dailySalesRepository.save(daily);
    }
}
Step 3: Customer Event Handler
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class CustomerEventHandler {
    
    @Autowired
    private Customer360Repository customer360Repository;
    
    @Autowired
    private OrderSummaryReportRepository orderRepository;
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "customer.events", groupId = "reporting-group")
    @Transactional
    public void handleCustomerEvent(CustomerEvent event) {
        log.info("Processing customer event: {} for customer: {}", 
                 event.getEventType(), event.getCustomerId());
        
        switch (event.getEventType()) {
            case "CUSTOMER_CREATED":
                Customer360Report customer = new Customer360Report();
                customer.setCustomerId(event.getCustomerId());
                customer.setCustomerName(event.getCustomerName());
                customer.setCustomerEmail(event.getCustomerEmail());
                customer.setCustomerPhone(event.getCustomerPhone());
                customer.setCustomerTier(event.getTier());
                customer.setRegistrationDate(event.getRegistrationDate());
                customer.setLastUpdated(LocalDateTime.now());
                customer360Repository.save(customer);
                break;
                
            case "CUSTOMER_UPDATED":
                customer360Repository.findById(event.getCustomerId()).ifPresent(c -> {
                    if (event.getCustomerName() != null) c.setCustomerName(event.getCustomerName());
                    if (event.getCustomerEmail() != null) c.setCustomerEmail(event.getCustomerEmail());
                    if (event.getCustomerPhone() != null) c.setCustomerPhone(event.getCustomerPhone());
                    if (event.getTier() != null) c.setCustomerTier(event.getTier());
                    c.setLastUpdated(LocalDateTime.now());
                    customer360Repository.save(c);
                    
                    // Update denormalized customer info in all orders
                    orderRepository.updateCustomerInfo(
                        event.getCustomerId(),
                        event.getCustomerName(),
                        event.getCustomerEmail()
                    );
                });
                break;
        }
    }
}
Step 4: Reporting API Endpoints
java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/reports")
@Slf4j
public class ReportController {
    
    @Autowired
    private OrderSummaryReportRepository orderReportRepository;
    
    @Autowired
    private Customer360Repository customer360Repository;
    
    @Autowired
    private DailySalesAggregateRepository dailySalesRepository;
    
    @GetMapping("/orders")
    public Page<OrderSummaryReport> getOrderReport(
            @RequestParam(required = false) LocalDate fromDate,
            @RequestParam(required = false) LocalDate toDate,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) UUID customerId,
            @PageableDefault(size = 50, sort = "orderDate", direction = Sort.Direction.DESC) Pageable pageable) {
        
        Specification<OrderSummaryReport> spec = Specification.where(null);
        
        if (fromDate != null && toDate != null) {
            spec = spec.and((root, query, cb) -> 
                cb.between(root.get("orderDate"), fromDate.atStartOfDay(), toDate.atTime(23, 59, 59)));
        }
        
        if (status != null) {
            spec = spec.and((root, query, cb) -> 
                cb.equal(root.get("orderStatus"), status));
        }
        
        if (customerId != null) {
            spec = spec.and((root, query, cb) -> 
                cb.equal(root.get("customerId"), customerId));
        }
        
        return orderReportRepository.findAll(spec, pageable);
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/customer-360/{customerId}")
    public Customer360Report getCustomer360(@PathVariable UUID customerId) {
        return customer360Repository.findById(customerId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("Customer not found"));
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/sales/daily")
    public List<DailySalesAggregate> getDailySales(
            @RequestParam LocalDate fromDate,
            @RequestParam LocalDate toDate) {
        return dailySalesRepository.findBySaleDateBetween(fromDate, toDate);
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/sales/summary")
    public SalesSummary getSalesSummary(
            @RequestParam LocalDate fromDate,
            @RequestParam LocalDate toDate) {
        
        List<DailySalesAggregate> dailySales = dailySalesRepository
            .findBySaleDateBetween(fromDate, toDate);
        
        SalesSummary summary = new SalesSummary();
        summary.setFromDate(fromDate);
        summary.setToDate(toDate);
        summary.setTotalOrders(dailySales.stream().mapToInt(DailySalesAggregate::getTotalOrders).sum());
        summary.setTotalRevenue(dailySales.stream().map(DailySalesAggregate::getTotalRevenue)
            .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add));
        summary.setAverageDailyOrders(summary.getTotalOrders() / (double) dailySales.size());
        summary.setAverageDailyRevenue(summary.getTotalRevenue().divide(
            BigDecimal.valueOf(dailySales.size()), 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
        
        return summary;
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/top-customers")
    public List<Customer360Report> getTopCustomers(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "10") int limit) {
        return customer360Repository.findTopByOrderByTotalSpentDesc(PageRequest.of(0, limit));
    }
}
Pattern 3: Change Data Capture (CDC) for Real-time Sync
For existing systems, use CDC to capture changes without modifying services.

java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class DebeziumEventListener {
    
    @EventListener
    public void handleDebeziumEvent(CDCChangeEvent event) {
        log.info("CDC event: table={}, operation={}", 
                 event.getTableName(), event.getOperation());
        
        switch (event.getOperation()) {
            case "c":  // Create
                handleCreate(event);
                break;
            case "u":  // Update
                handleUpdate(event);
                break;
            case "d":  // Delete
                handleDelete(event);
                break;
        }
    }
    
    private void handleCreate(CDCChangeEvent event) {
        Map<String, Object> after = event.getAfter();
        
        switch (event.getTableName()) {
            case "orders":
                OrderSummaryReport report = mapToReport(after);
                reportRepository.save(report);
                break;
            case "customers":
                Customer360Report customer = mapToCustomer(after);
                customer360Repository.save(customer);
                break;
        }
    }
}
Pattern 4: Precomputed Aggregates with Batch Processing
For large-scale analytics, precompute aggregates periodically.

java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class BatchAggregationJob {
    
    @Autowired
    private JdbcTemplate reportingJdbcTemplate;
    
    @Autowired
    private OrderServiceClient orderClient;
    
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 2 * * *")  // Run at 2 AM daily
    @Transactional
    public void computeDailyAggregates() {
        LocalDate yesterday = LocalDate.now().minusDays(1);
        log.info("Computing daily aggregates for: {}", yesterday);
        
        // Fetch data from multiple services
        List<OrderDTO> orders = orderClient.getOrdersByDate(yesterday);
        List<PaymentDTO> payments = paymentClient.getPaymentsByDate(yesterday);
        List<CustomerDTO> newCustomers = customerClient.getNewCustomers(yesterday);
        
        // Compute aggregates
        DailyReportAggregate aggregate = new DailyReportAggregate();
        aggregate.setDate(yesterday);
        aggregate.setTotalOrders(orders.size());
        aggregate.setTotalRevenue(calculateTotalRevenue(payments));
        aggregate.setNewCustomers(newCustomers.size());
        aggregate.setAverageOrderValue(calculateAverageOrderValue(orders));
        aggregate.setTopProducts(findTopProducts(orders));
        
        // Save to reporting database
        reportingJdbcTemplate.update(
            "INSERT INTO daily_aggregates (date, total_orders, total_revenue, new_customers, avg_order_value, top_products) " +
            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) " +
            "ON CONFLICT (date) DO UPDATE SET total_orders = EXCLUDED.total_orders, total_revenue = EXCLUDED.total_revenue",
            aggregate.getDate(), aggregate.getTotalOrders(), aggregate.getTotalRevenue(),
            aggregate.getNewCustomers(), aggregate.getAverageOrderValue(),
            aggregate.getTopProducts().toString()
        );
        
        log.info("Daily aggregates computed for: {}", yesterday);
    }
}
Pattern 5: Query Federation with GraphQL
For complex reporting with flexible querying, use GraphQL federation.

java
@Controller
@Slf4j
public class ReportGraphQLController {
    
    @QueryMapping
    public CompletableFuture<CustomerReport> customerReport(
            @Argument Long customerId,
            @Argument DateRange dateRange) {
        
        // GraphQL automatically resolves fields from different services
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            CustomerReport report = new CustomerReport();
            report.setCustomerId(customerId);
            report.setDateRange(dateRange);
            return report;
        });
    }
}

// Federated GraphQL schema
// type Customer @key(fields: "id") {
//   id: ID!
//   name: String!
//   email: String!
//   orders: [Order]
//   payments: [Payment]
//   totalSpent: Float
// }
//
// type Order @key(fields: "id") {
//   id: ID!
//   customerId: ID!
//   total: Float!
//   status: String!
//   date: Date!
// }
Pattern Comparison
Pattern	Complexity	Latency	Data Freshness	Use Case
API Composition	Low	High	Real-time	Simple reports, small data
CQRS/Reporting DB	Medium	Low	Near real-time	Most reporting (recommended)
Change Data Capture	Medium	Low	Real-time	Existing systems, minimal changes
Batch Aggregates	Low-Medium	Medium	Daily	Large-scale analytics, dashboards
Data Lake	High	High	Batch	Advanced analytics, ML
GraphQL Federation	Medium	Variable	Real-time	Flexible queries, API consumers
Production Checklist
Choose reporting pattern based on data volume and freshness requirements

Implement idempotent event handlers for reliable processing

Use denormalized tables for query performance

Add appropriate indexes for common report queries

Implement pagination for large result sets

Cache frequently accessed reports (Redis)

Monitor reporting service performance

Implement data retention policies

Add export functionality (CSV, Excel, PDF)

Secure reporting endpoints with proper authorization

The CQRS with Reporting Database pattern is generally the best choice for most reporting scenarios, offering a good balance of performance, complexity, and data freshness.

How to automate on prem microservices with diagram and complete explanation?
Automating On-Prem Microservices: Complete Guide
Automating on-premise microservices is fundamentally different from cloud automation. You don't have cloud-native services like EKS, AKS, or Lambda. Instead, you must build your own automation platform using open-source tools. This guide provides a complete, production-ready approach.

The Core Challenge
text
CLOUD NATIVE                          ON-PREMISE
┌─────────────────────┐              ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  AWS/Azure/GCP      │              │  YOUR DATA CENTER                   │
├─────────────────────┤              ├─────────────────────────────────────┤
│  ✓ Managed K8s      │              │  ✗ Self-managed K8s (or VMs)        │
│  ✓ Managed Registry │              │  ✗ Self-managed Registry            │
│  ✓ Managed CI/CD    │              │  ✗ Self-managed CI/CD               │
│  ✓ Managed LB       │              │  ✗ Self-managed LB/Ingress          │
│  ✓ Managed Logging  │              │  ✗ Self-managed ELK Stack           │
│  ✓ Managed Monitoring│             │  ✗ Self-managed Prometheus          │
└─────────────────────┘              └─────────────────────────────────────┘

Step 1: Infrastructure Automation (Terraform)
VM Provisioning for On-Prem
hcl
# terraform/main.tf
terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.0"
  
  backend "http" {
    address = "http://your-artifactory/terraform/state"
  }
}

provider "vsphere" {
  vsphere_server       = "vcenter.yourcompany.com"
  user                 = var.vsphere_user
  password             = var.vsphere_password
  allow_unverified_ssl = true
}

# Kubernetes Master Nodes
resource "vsphere_virtual_machine" "k8s_master" {
  count            = 3
  name             = "k8s-master-${count.index + 1}"
  resource_pool_id = data.vsphere_resource_pool.pool.id
  datastore_id     = data.vsphere_datastore.datastore.id
  
  num_cpus = 4
  memory   = 8192
  guest_id = "ubuntu64Guest"
  
  network_interface {
    network_id   = data.vsphere_network.network.id
    adapter_type = "vmxnet3"
  }
  
  disk {
    label            = "disk0"
    size             = 100
    eagerly_scrub    = false
    thin_provisioned = true
  }
  
  provisioner "remote-exec" {
    inline = [
      "sudo apt-get update",
      "sudo apt-get install -y docker.io kubeadm kubelet kubectl",
      "sudo systemctl start docker",
      "sudo systemctl enable docker"
    ]
  }
}

# Kubernetes Worker Nodes
resource "vsphere_virtual_machine" "k8s_worker" {
  count            = var.worker_count
  name             = "k8s-worker-${count.index + 1}"
  resource_pool_id = data.vsphere_resource_pool.pool.id
  datastore_id     = data.vsphere_datastore.datastore.id
  
  num_cpus = 8
  memory   = 16384
  guest_id = "ubuntu64Guest"
  
  network_interface {
    network_id   = data.vsphere_network.network.id
    adapter_type = "vmxnet3"
  }
  
  disk {
    label            = "disk0"
    size             = 200
    eagerly_scrub    = false
    thin_provisioned = true
  }
}

# Load Balancer VM (HAProxy)
resource "vsphere_virtual_machine" "lb" {
  name             = "load-balancer"
  resource_pool_id = data.vsphere_resource_pool.pool.id
  datastore_id     = data.vsphere_datastore.datastore.id
  
  num_cpus = 2
  memory   = 4096
  
  provisioner "remote-exec" {
    script = "scripts/haproxy-setup.sh"
  }
}

# Storage VM (NFS for PVs)
resource "vsphere_virtual_machine" "storage" {
  name             = "storage-server"
  resource_pool_id = data.vsphere_resource_pool.pool.id
  datastore_id     = data.vsphere_datastore.datastore.id
  
  num_cpus = 4
  memory   = 8192
  
  disk {
    label = "data-disk"
    size  = 1000  # 1TB for persistent volumes
  }
  
  provisioner "remote-exec" {
    script = "scripts/nfs-setup.sh"
  }
}
Step 2: Kubernetes Cluster Setup (Ansible)
Ansible Playbook for K8s Cluster
yaml
# ansible/k8s-cluster-setup.yml
---
- name: Setup Kubernetes Cluster
  hosts: all
  become: yes
  vars:
    k8s_version: "1.28.0"
    pod_network_cidr: "10.244.0.0/16"
    service_cidr: "10.96.0.0/12"
    
  tasks:
    - name: Disable swap
      command: swapoff -a
      when: ansible_swaptotal_mb > 0
      
    - name: Remove swap from fstab
      lineinfile:
        path: /etc/fstab
        regexp: 'swap'
        state: absent
        
    - name: Load kernel modules
      modprobe:
        name: "{{ item }}"
        state: present
      loop:
        - overlay
        - br_netfilter
        
    - name: Configure sysctl for Kubernetes
      sysctl:
        name: "{{ item.name }}"
        value: "{{ item.value }}"
        state: present
        reload: yes
      loop:
        - { name: net.bridge.bridge-nf-call-iptables, value: 1 }
        - { name: net.ipv4.ip_forward, value: 1 }
        - { name: net.bridge.bridge-nf-call-ip6tables, value: 1 }
        
    - name: Install Docker
      apt:
        name:
          - docker.io
          - docker-compose
        state: present
        update_cache: yes
        
    - name: Install Kubernetes components
      apt:
        name:
          - kubeadm={{ k8s_version }}
          - kubelet={{ k8s_version }}
          - kubectl={{ k8s_version }}
        state: present
        
    - name: Hold Kubernetes packages
      dpkg_selections:
        name: "{{ item }}"
        selection: hold
      loop:
        - kubeadm
        - kubelet
        - kubectl
        
    - name: Initialize master node (first master only)
      command: |
        kubeadm init --pod-network-cidr={{ pod_network_cidr }} \
                     --service-cidr={{ service_cidr }} \
                     --control-plane-endpoint=load-balancer:6443
      when: inventory_hostname == groups['masters'][0]
      
    - name: Create .kube directory
      file:
        path: /root/.kube
        state: directory
        mode: '0755'
        
    - name: Copy admin kubeconfig
      command: cp /etc/kubernetes/admin.conf /root/.kube/config
      when: inventory_hostname == groups['masters'][0]
      
    - name: Install Calico CNI
      command: kubectl apply -f https://docs.projectcalico.org/manifests/calico.yaml
      when: inventory_hostname == groups['masters'][0]
      
    - name: Get join command
      command: kubeadm token create --print-join-command
      register: join_command
      when: inventory_hostname == groups['masters'][0]
      
    - name: Join worker nodes
      command: "{{ hostvars[groups['masters'][0]].join_command.stdout }}"
      when: inventory_hostname in groups['workers']
Step 3: CI/CD Pipeline (Jenkins)
Jenkins Pipeline for Microservice Deployment
groovy
// Jenkinsfile
pipeline {
    agent any
    
    environment {
        REGISTRY = 'harbor.yourcompany.com'
        REGISTRY_CREDENTIALS = 'harbor-credentials'
        K8S_NAMESPACE = 'microservices'
        SONAR_HOST = 'http://sonarqube.yourcompany.com'
    }
    
    parameters {
        choice(name: 'SERVICE', choices: ['order-service', 'payment-service', 'inventory-service'], description: 'Service to deploy')
        choice(name: 'ENVIRONMENT', choices: ['dev', 'staging', 'production'], description: 'Target environment')
        string(name: 'VERSION', defaultValue: 'latest', description: 'Image version/tag')
    }
    
    stages {
        stage('Checkout') {
            steps {
                checkout scm
                script {
                    serviceName = params.SERVICE
                    version = params.VERSION == 'latest' ? "v${env.BUILD_NUMBER}" : params.VERSION
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Build & Test') {
            parallel {
                stage('Compile') {
                    steps {
                        dir("${serviceName}") {
                            sh """
                                ./gradlew clean compileJava
                            """
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                stage('Unit Tests') {
                    steps {
                        dir("${serviceName}") {
                            sh """
                                ./gradlew test
                            """
                        }
                    }
                    post {
                        always {
                            junit("${serviceName}/build/test-results/test/*.xml")
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                stage('Integration Tests') {
                    steps {
                        dir("${serviceName}") {
                            sh """
                                ./gradlew integrationTest
                            """
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                stage('Code Quality') {
                    steps {
                        dir("${serviceName}") {
                            sh """
                                ./gradlew sonarqube \\
                                    -Dsonar.host.url=${SONAR_HOST} \\
                                    -Dsonar.projectKey=${serviceName} \\
                                    -Dsonar.branch.name=${env.BRANCH_NAME}
                            """
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Security Scan') {
            steps {
                script {
                    // Dependency scan
                    sh """
                        trivy filesystem --exit-code 1 --severity CRITICAL ${serviceName}/
                    """
                    
                    // Container scan
                    sh """
                        grype ${serviceName}:${version} --fail-on high
                    """
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Build Container Image') {
            steps {
                dir("${serviceName}") {
                    sh """
                        docker build -t ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:${version} .
                        docker tag ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:${version} ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:latest
                    """
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Push to Registry') {
            steps {
                withCredentials([usernamePassword(credentialsId: REGISTRY_CREDENTIALS, 
                                                  usernameVariable: 'REG_USER', 
                                                  passwordVariable: 'REG_PASS')]) {
                    sh """
                        echo ${REG_PASS} | docker login ${REGISTRY} -u ${REG_USER} --password-stdin
                        docker push ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:${version}
                        docker push ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:latest
                    """
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Update GitOps Manifests') {
            steps {
                script {
                    // Update K8s deployment manifest
                    sh """
                        git clone git@gitlab.yourcompany.com:gitops/manifests.git
                        cd manifests
                        sed -i "s|image: ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:.*|image: ${REGISTRY}/${serviceName}:${version}|g" \
                            ${K8S_NAMESPACE}/${serviceName}/deployment.yaml
                        git add .
                        git commit -m "Update ${serviceName} to version ${version} [skip ci]"
                        git push origin main
                    """
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Deploy with ArgoCD') {
            steps {
                script {
                    // Wait for ArgoCD to sync
                    sh """
                        argocd app sync ${serviceName}-${params.ENVIRONMENT} \
                            --grpc-web \
                            --timeout 300
                    """
                    
                    // Wait for rollout
                    sh """
                        kubectl rollout status deployment/${serviceName} \
                            -n ${K8S_NAMESPACE} \
                            --timeout=5m
                    """
                }
            }
        }
        
        stage('Smoke Tests') {
            steps {
                script {
                    // Run post-deployment validation
                    sh """
                        curl -f https://api.yourcompany.com/${serviceName}/health || exit 1
                    """
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    post {
        success {
            emailext(
                subject: "SUCCESS: ${serviceName} deployed",
                body: "Version ${version} deployed to ${params.ENVIRONMENT}",
                to: "team@yourcompany.com"
            )
        }
        failure {
            emailext(
                subject: "FAILED: ${serviceName} deployment",
                body: "Deployment failed. Check Jenkins console for details.",
                to: "team@yourcompany.com"
            )
        }
    }
}

Service Deployment with Best Practices
yaml
# k8s/microservices/order-service/deployment.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: order-service
  namespace: microservices
  labels:
    app: order-service
    version: v1
spec:
  replicas: 3
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: order-service
        version: v1
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: "8080"
        prometheus.io/path: "/actuator/prometheus"
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: order-service
        image: harbor.yourcompany.com/order-service:latest
        imagePullPolicy: Always
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8080
          name: http
        - containerPort: 9779
          name: prometheus
        env:
        - name: SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE
          value: "production"
        - name: DB_HOST
          valueFrom:
            configMapKeyRef:
              name: microservices-config
              key: order.db.host
        - name: DB_PASSWORD
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: db-secrets
              key: order.db.password
        - name: KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS
          value: "kafka-headless:9092"
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "512Mi"
            cpu: "250m"
          limits:
            memory: "1Gi"
            cpu: "500m"
        livenessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /actuator/health/liveness
            port: 8080
          initialDelaySeconds: 60
          periodSeconds: 10
        readinessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /actuator/health/readiness
            port: 8080
          initialDelaySeconds: 30
          periodSeconds: 5
        startupProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /actuator/health
            port: 8080
          failureThreshold: 30
          periodSeconds: 10
        volumeMounts:
        - name: config
          mountPath: /app/config
        - name: logs
          mountPath: /var/log/app
      volumes:
      - name: config
        configMap:
          name: order-service-config
      - name: logs
        emptyDir: {}
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 60
---
# Service
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: order-service
  namespace: microservices
  labels:
    app: order-service
spec:
  selector:
    app: order-service
  ports:
  - name: http
    port: 8080
    targetPort: 8080
  - name: prometheus
    port: 9779
    targetPort: 9779
  type: ClusterIP
---
# Horizontal Pod Autoscaler
apiVersion: autoscaling/v2
kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: order-service-hpa
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  scaleTargetRef:
    apiVersion: apps/v1
    kind: Deployment
    name: order-service
  minReplicas: 3
  maxReplicas: 10
  metrics:
  - type: Resource
    resource:
      name: cpu
      target:
        type: Utilization
        averageUtilization: 70
  - type: Resource
    resource:
      name: memory
      target:
        type: Utilization
        averageUtilization: 80
---
# Pod Disruption Budget
apiVersion: policy/v1
kind: PodDisruptionBudget
metadata:
  name: order-service-pdb
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  minAvailable: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service

# k8s/ingress/nginx-ingress.yaml
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: microservices-ingress
  namespace: microservices
  annotations:
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rewrite-target: /$2
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/ssl-redirect: "true"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-body-size: "10m"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-read-timeout: "60"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-send-timeout: "60"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rate-limit: "100"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rate-limit-burst: "200"
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: "letsencrypt-prod"
spec:
  tls:
  - hosts:
    - api.yourcompany.com
    secretName: api-tls
  rules:
  - host: api.yourcompany.com
    http:
      paths:
      - path: /api/v1/orders(/|$)(.*)
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: order-service
            port:
              number: 8080
      - path: /api/v1/payments(/|$)(.*)
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: payment-service
            port:
              number: 8080

      cpu: 2
    limits:
      memory: 16Gi
      cpu: 4
  retention: 30d
  retentionSize: 100GB
  ruleSelector:
    matchLabels:
      prometheus: microservices
  alerting:

    - namespace: monitoring
      name: alertmanager
      port: web

  name: microservices-monitor

  labels:
    team: microservices
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  endpoints:
  - port: prometheus
    interval: 30s
    path: /actuator/prometheus
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames:
    - microservices
Alerting Rules
yaml
# prometheus/alert-rules.yaml
groups:
- name: microservices-alerts
  rules:
  - alert: HighErrorRate
    expr: |
      sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{status=~"5.."}[5m])) 
      / 
      sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count[5m])) > 0.05
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: critical
      team: microservices
    annotations:
      summary: "High error rate in {{ $labels.service }}"
      description: "Error rate is {{ $value }}% for the last 5 minutes"
      
  - alert: ServiceDown
    expr: up{job="microservices"} == 0
    for: 1m
    labels:
      severity: critical
    annotations:
      summary: "Service {{ $labels.instance }} is down"
      description: "{{ $labels.instance }} has been down for more than 1 minute"
      
  - alert: HighLatency
    expr: |
      histogram_quantile(0.95, 
        sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_bucket[5m])) by (le, service)
      ) > 2
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: warning
    annotations:
      summary: "High latency in {{ $labels.service }}"
      description: "95th percentile latency is {{ $value }}s"
      
  - alert: PodCrashLooping
    expr: |
      rate(kube_pod_container_status_restarts_total[15m]) > 0
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: warning
    annotations:
      summary: "Pod {{ $labels.pod }} is crash looping"
      description: "Pod {{ $labels.pod }} has restarted {{ $value }} times in 15 minutes"

  name: microservices-backup
  namespace: velero
spec:
  schedule: "0 2 * * *"  # Daily at 2 AM
  template:
    includedNamespaces:
    - microservices
    includedResources:
    - deployments
    - services
    - configmaps
    - secrets
    - persistentvolumeclaims
    ttl: 720h  # 30 days retention
    storageLocation: default
    volumeSnapshotLocations:
    - default
---

metadata:
  name: nfs-backup
  namespace: velero
spec:
  provider: velero.io/aws
  objectStorage:
    bucket: /backups/velero
  config:
    region: minio
    s3Url: http://minio.velero.svc:9000
    s3ForcePathStyle: "true"
Step 7: Database Automation
PostgreSQL Operator (Crunchy Data)
yaml
# postgres/postgres-cluster.yaml
apiVersion: postgres-operator.crunchydata.com/v1beta1
kind: PostgresCluster
metadata:
  name: order-db
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  image: registry.developers.crunchydata.com/crunchydata/crunchy-postgres:ubi8-14.5-0
  postgresVersion: 14
  instances:
    - name: primary
      replicas: 3
      dataVolumeClaimSpec:
        accessModes:
        - "ReadWriteOnce"
        resources:
          requests:
            storage: 100Gi
      affinity:
        podAntiAffinity:
          preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
          - weight: 1
            podAffinityTerm:
              topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
              labelSelector:
                matchLabels:
                  postgres-operator.crunchydata.com/cluster: order-db
                  postgres-operator.crunchydata.com/instance: primary
  backups:
    pgbackrest:
      image: registry.developers.crunchydata.com/crunchydata/crunchy-pgbackrest:ubi8-2.38-0
      repos:
      - name: repo1
        volume:
          volumeClaimSpec:
            accessModes:
            - "ReadWriteOnce"
            resources:
              requests:
                storage: 200Gi
        schedules:
          full: "0 1 * * *"      # Daily full backup
          differential: "0 */6 * * *"  # Every 6 hours
          incremental: "*/30 * * * *"  # Every 30 minutes
  proxy:
    pgBouncer:
      replicas: 2
      config:
        global:
          max_client_conn: "1000"
          default_pool_size: "100"
Step 8: Complete Automation Script
bash
#!/bin/bash
# deploy-microservices.sh - Complete deployment automation

set -e

# Colors for output
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
NC='\033[0m'

log_info() { echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO]${NC} $1"; }
log_warn() { echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN]${NC} $1"; }
log_error() { echo -e "${RED}[ERROR]${NC} $1"; }

# Configuration
ENVIRONMENT=${1:-"dev"}
VERSION=${2:-"latest"}
NAMESPACE="microservices-${ENVIRONMENT}"

# Pre-flight checks
check_prerequisites() {
    log_info "Checking prerequisites..."
    
    commands=("kubectl" "helm" "terraform" "argocd" "docker")
    for cmd in "${commands[@]}"; do
        if ! command -v $cmd &> /dev/null; then
            log_error "$cmd is not installed"
            exit 1
        fi
    done
    
    if ! kubectl cluster-info &> /dev/null; then
        log_error "Kubernetes cluster not accessible"
        exit 1
    fi
    
    log_info "All prerequisites satisfied"
}

# Deploy infrastructure
deploy_infrastructure() {
    log_info "Deploying infrastructure components..."
    
    # Deploy ingress controller
    helm upgrade --install ingress-nginx ingress-nginx \
        --repo https://kubernetes.github.io/ingress-nginx \
        --namespace ingress-nginx --create-namespace
    
    # Deploy cert-manager
    helm upgrade --install cert-manager cert-manager \
        --repo https://charts.jetstack.io \
        --namespace cert-manager --create-namespace \
        --set installCRDs=true
    
    # Deploy Prometheus stack
    helm upgrade --install prometheus prometheus-community/kube-prometheus-stack \
        --namespace monitoring --create-namespace \
        -f monitoring/values.yaml
    
    # Deploy ELK stack
    helm upgrade --install elasticsearch elastic/elasticsearch \
        --namespace logging --create-namespace \
        --set replicas=3
    
    # Deploy Kafka
    helm upgrade --install kafka bitnami/kafka \
        --namespace kafka --create-namespace \
        -f kafka/values.yaml
}

# Deploy microservices
deploy_microservices() {
    log_info "Deploying microservices to ${ENVIRONMENT}..."
    
    # Create namespace
    kubectl create namespace ${NAMESPACE} --dry-run=client -o yaml | kubectl apply -f -
    
    # Apply secrets
    kubectl apply -f secrets/${ENVIRONMENT}/ -n ${NAMESPACE}
    
    # Apply ConfigMaps
    kubectl apply -f configmaps/${ENVIRONMENT}/ -n ${NAMESPACE}
    
    # Deploy services in order
    services=("database" "redis" "order-service" "payment-service" "inventory-service" "api-gateway")
    
    for service in "${services[@]}"; do
        log_info "Deploying ${service}..."
        kubectl apply -f services/${service}/${ENVIRONMENT}/ -n ${NAMESPACE}
        
        # Wait for deployment to be ready
        if [[ -f "services/${service}/${ENVIRONMENT}/deployment.yaml" ]]; then
            kubectl rollout status deployment/${service} -n ${NAMESPACE} --timeout=5m
        fi
    done
    
    # Apply ingress
    kubectl apply -f ingress/${ENVIRONMENT}/ -n ${NAMESPACE}
}

# Run smoke tests
run_smoke_tests() {
    log_info "Running smoke tests..."
    
    # Get ingress IP
    INGRESS_IP=$(kubectl get ingress -n ${NAMESPACE} -o jsonpath='{.items[0].status.loadBalancer.ingress[0].ip}')
    
    # Test endpoints
    endpoints=("/health" "/actuator/health" "/api/v1/orders/health")
    
    for endpoint in "${endpoints[@]}"; do
        log_info "Testing ${endpoint}..."
        if curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" http://${INGRESS_IP}${endpoint} | grep -q "200"; then
            log_info "✓ ${endpoint} is healthy"
        else
            log_error "✗ ${endpoint} failed"
            exit 1
        fi
    done
}

# Setup monitoring
setup_monitoring() {
    log_info "Setting up monitoring and dashboards..."
    
    # Import Grafana dashboards
    for dashboard in dashboards/*.json; do
        curl -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
            -d @${dashboard} \
            http://grafana.monitoring.svc:3000/api/dashboards/db
    done
    
    # Setup alerting
    kubectl apply -f alerts/${ENVIRONMENT}/ -n monitoring
}

# Main execution
main() {
    log_info "Starting deployment for environment: ${ENVIRONMENT}"
    
    case "${ENVIRONMENT}" in
        dev)
            deploy_infrastructure
            deploy_microservices
            run_smoke_tests
            setup_monitoring
            ;;
        staging)
            deploy_microservices
            run_smoke_tests
            ;;
        production)
            log_warn "Production deployment requires approval"
            read -p "Are you sure? (y/n) " -n 1 -r
            echo
            if [[ $REPLY =~ ^[Yy]$ ]]; then
                deploy_microservices
                run_smoke_tests
                setup_monitoring
            fi
            ;;
        *)
            log_error "Invalid environment: ${ENVIRONMENT}"
            exit 1
            ;;
    esac
    
    log_info "Deployment completed successfully!"
}

# Run with error handling
check_prerequisites
main "$@"
Step 9: GitOps with ArgoCD
Application Configuration
yaml
# argocd/applications.yaml
apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
kind: Application
metadata:
  name: microservices-dev
  namespace: argocd
spec:
  project: default
  source:
    repoURL: git@gitlab.yourcompany.com/gitops/manifests.git
    targetRevision: HEAD
    path: environments/dev
    directory:
      recurse: true
  destination:
    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
    namespace: microservices-dev
  syncPolicy:
    automated:
      prune: true
      selfHeal: true
    syncOptions:
    - CreateNamespace=true
    - PrunePropagationPolicy=foreground
    retry:
      limit: 5
      backoff:
        duration: 5s
        factor: 2
        maxDuration: 3m
---
apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
kind: Application
metadata:
  name: microservices-prod
  namespace: argocd
spec:
  project: default
  source:
    repoURL: git@gitlab.yourcompany.com/gitops/manifests.git
    targetRevision: production
    path: environments/prod
  destination:
    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
    namespace: microservices-prod
  syncPolicy:
    automated:
      prune: true
      selfHeal: true
Step 10: Service Mesh with Istio (Optional but Recommended)
yaml
# istio/virtual-service.yaml
apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: VirtualService
metadata:
  name: order-service
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  hosts:
  - order-service
  http:
  - match:
    - headers:
        canary:
          exact: "true"
    route:
    - destination:
        host: order-service
        subset: v2
      weight: 10
    - destination:
        host: order-service
        subset: v1
      weight: 90
  - route:
    - destination:
        host: order-service
        subset: v1
      weight: 100
---
apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: DestinationRule
metadata:
  name: order-service
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  host: order-service
  trafficPolicy:
    connectionPool:
      tcp:
        maxConnections: 100
      http:
        http1MaxPendingRequests: 10
        http2MaxRequests: 100
    loadBalancer:
      simple: ROUND_ROBIN
    outlierDetection:
      consecutive5xxErrors: 5
      interval: 30s
      baseEjectionTime: 30s
  subsets:
  - name: v1
    labels:
      version: v1
  - name: v2
    labels:
      version: v2

Metric	Target	Monitoring Tool
Deployment Time	< 5 minutes	Jenkins/ArgoCD
Rollback Time	< 2 minutes	ArgoCD
Success Rate	> 99%	Prometheus
Mean Time to Recovery	< 30 minutes	PagerDuty/Prometheus
Infrastructure as Code Coverage	> 90%	Terraform
GitOps Sync Status	100%	ArgoCD
Production Checklist
Infrastructure: VMs provisioned with Terraform, K8s cluster configured

CI/CD: Jenkins pipeline with security scanning, artifact promotion

Registry: Harbor with vulnerability scanning, replication

GitOps: ArgoCD with auto-sync, drift detection

Observability: Prometheus, Grafana, Loki, Tempo configured

Service Mesh: Istio with mTLS, traffic management

Backup: Velero with scheduled backups, disaster recovery tested

Security: RBAC, network policies, OPA/Gatekeeper

Scaling: HPA, cluster autoscaler, resource quotas

Cost Management: Resource requests/limits, cost monitoring

Documentation: Runbooks, architecture diagrams, on-call guides

This complete automation stack gives you enterprise-grade on-premise microservice automation comparable to cloud-native platforms!

How to approch cloud based microsercices infrastructure?
Cloud-Based Microservices Infrastructure: Complete Approach
Cloud-based microservices infrastructure is fundamentally different from on-premise because you leverage managed services instead of building everything yourself. The key principle is "you build it, you run it, but the cloud operates the underlying infrastructure."

Cloud vs On-Prem: The Paradigm Shift
text
ON-PREMISE MENTALITY                    CLOUD-NATIVE MENTALITY
┌─────────────────────────┐            ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│ "How do I build this?"  │            │ "Which managed service should I use?"│
├─────────────────────────┤            ├─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ • Self-managed K8s      │            │ • EKS/AKS/GKE (managed K8s)         │
│ • Self-managed Registry │            │ • ECR/ACR/GAR (managed registry)    │
│ • Self-managed LB       │            │ • ALB/Cloud LB (managed LB)         │
│ • Self-managed Storage  │            │ • S3/Blob/GCS (managed storage)     │
│ • Self-managed Database │            │ • RDS/Cloud SQL (managed DB)        │
│ • Self-managed Kafka    │            │ • MSK/Event Hubs/PubSub (managed)   │
│ • Self-managed Logging  │            │ • CloudWatch/Log Analytics          │
└─────────────────────────┘            └─────────────────────────────────────┘

COST STRUCTURE                          COST STRUCTURE
┌─────────────────────────┐            ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│ High upfront (hardware) │            │ Pay-as-you-go (no upfront)          │
│ Fixed capacity          │            │ Auto-scaling                         │
│ Ops team expensive      │            │ Less ops (managed services)         │
│ Predictable but high    │            │ Variable but potentially lower      │
└─────────────────────────┘            └─────────────────────────────────────┘
Complete Cloud Microservices Architecture (AWS Example)

│  │   (Logs)     │  │   (Audit)    │                                                 │

Multi-Cloud vs Single Cloud: Strategic Decision
text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         CLOUD STRATEGY COMPARISON                                    │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                                      │
│  SINGLE CLOUD (AWS, Azure, or GCP)          MULTI-CLOUD                             │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────┐       ┌─────────────────────────────────┐     │
│  │ ✅ Best integration             │       │ ✅ No vendor lock-in            │     │
│  │ ✅ Simplified operations        │       │ ✅ Best price for each service  │     │
│  │ ✅ Single billing/support       │       │ ✅ Disaster recovery across      │     │
│  │ ✅ Native service mesh          │       │    cloud providers              │     │
│  │ ✅ Faster development           │       │ ✅ Regulatory compliance        │     │
│  │ ✅ Lower operational cost       │       │    (data residency)             │     │
│  └─────────────────────────────────┘       └─────────────────────────────────┘     │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────┐       ┌─────────────────────────────────┐     │
│  │ ❌ Vendor lock-in               │       │ ❌ Higher complexity            │     │
│  │ ❌ Single point of failure      │       │ ❌ Higher cost (data transfer)  │     │
│  │ ❌ Regional outage risk         │       │ ❌ Inconsistent services        │     │
│  └─────────────────────────────────┘       │ ❌ Multiple billing/support     │     │
│                                            │ ❌ Cross-cloud networking       │     │
│  RECOMMENDATION: Start Single Cloud        │    complexity                   │     │
│  then evolve to Multi-Cloud if needed      └─────────────────────────────────┘     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
Step 1: Infrastructure as Code (Terraform on AWS)
Multi-Region/Account Structure
hcl
# terraform/main.tf
terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.0"
  
  backend "s3" {
    bucket         = "terraform-state-production"
    key            = "microservices/terraform.tfstate"
    region         = "us-east-1"
    dynamodb_table = "terraform-locks"
    encrypt        = true
  }
}

provider "aws" {
  region = var.aws_region
  
  default_tags {
    tags = {
      Environment = var.environment
      Project     = "microservices"
      ManagedBy   = "Terraform"
      CostCenter  = "engineering"
    }
  }
}

# Multi-account strategy (AWS Organizations)
# Root: Management/Payer
# ├── Shared Services (Networking, CI/CD, Security)
# ├── Development (dev environment)
# ├── Staging (staging environment)
# └── Production (prod environment)

module "vpc" {
  source = "terraform-aws-modules/vpc/aws"
  version = "~> 5.0"

  name = "microservices-${var.environment}"
  cidr = var.vpc_cidr

  azs             = var.availability_zones
  private_subnets = var.private_subnet_cidrs
  public_subnets  = var.public_subnet_cidrs
  
  enable_nat_gateway     = true
  single_nat_gateway     = var.environment != "production"
  enable_vpn_gateway     = var.environment == "production"
  enable_dns_hostnames   = true
  enable_dns_support     = true
  
  # Tags for subnet identification
  private_subnet_tags = {
    "kubernetes.io/role/internal-elb" = "1"
    "karpenter.sh/discovery" = var.cluster_name
  }
  
  public_subnet_tags = {
    "kubernetes.io/role/elb" = "1"
    "karpenter.sh/discovery" = var.cluster_name
  }
}

# EKS Cluster (Managed Kubernetes)
module "eks" {
  source = "terraform-aws-modules/eks/aws"
  version = "~> 19.0"

  cluster_name    = "microservices-${var.environment}"
  cluster_version = "1.28"
  
  vpc_id     = module.vpc.vpc_id
  subnet_ids = module.vpc.private_subnets
  
  # Control plane logging
  cluster_enabled_log_types = ["api", "audit", "authenticator", "controllerManager", "scheduler"]
  
  # Managed node groups
  eks_managed_node_groups = {
    general = {
      desired_size = var.environment == "production" ? 3 : 2
      min_size     = var.environment == "production" ? 2 : 1
      max_size     = var.environment == "production" ? 20 : 5
      
      instance_types = ["m5.large", "m5.xlarge"]
      capacity_type  = "ON_DEMAND"
      
      labels = {
        workload-type = "general"
        environment   = var.environment
      }
      
      tags = {
        "k8s.io/cluster-autoscaler/enabled" = "true"
        "k8s.io/cluster-autoscaler/${var.environment}" = "owned"
      }
    }
    
    spot = {
      desired_size = 1
      min_size     = 1
      max_size     = 10
      
      instance_types = ["m5.large", "m5.xlarge", "m5.2xlarge"]
      capacity_type  = "SPOT"
      
      labels = {
        workload-type = "batch"
        spot-enabled  = "true"
      }
      
      taints = [{
        key    = "spotInstance"
        value  = "true"
        effect = "NO_SCHEDULE"
      }]
    }
  }
  
  # Fargate profiles (serverless containers)
  fargate_profiles = {
    critical = {
      name = "critical-workloads"
      selectors = [
        {
          namespace = "critical"
          labels = {
            "fargate" = "true"
          }
        }
      ]
    }
    
    monitoring = {
      name = "monitoring"
      selectors = [
        {
          namespace = "monitoring"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}

# ECR Repositories
resource "aws_ecr_repository" "microservices" {
  for_each = toset(var.microservices)
  
  name = "microservices/${each.value}"
  
  image_scanning_configuration {
    scan_on_push = true
  }
  
  encryption_configuration {
    encryption_type = "KMS"
    kms_key         = aws_kms_key.ecr.arn
  }
}

# RDS (PostgreSQL) - Managed Database
module "rds" {
  source = "terraform-aws-modules/rds/aws"
  version = "~> 6.0"

  identifier = "microservices-${var.environment}"
  
  engine               = "postgres"
  engine_version       = "15.3"
  family               = "postgres15"
  major_engine_version = "15"
  instance_class       = var.environment == "production" ? "db.r6g.large" : "db.t4g.large"
  
  storage_encrypted   = true
  storage_type        = "gp3"
  allocated_storage   = var.environment == "production" ? 200 : 50
  max_allocated_storage = var.environment == "production" ? 500 : 100
  
  db_name  = "microservices"
  username = "microservices_admin"
  
  port = 5432
  
  multi_az               = var.environment == "production"
  publicly_accessible    = false
  backup_window          = "03:00-04:00"
  backup_retention_period = var.environment == "production" ? 30 : 7
  
  # Performance Insights
  performance_insights_enabled          = var.environment == "production"
  performance_insights_retention_period = 7
  
  # Monitoring
  monitoring_interval = var.environment == "production" ? 60 : 0
  monitoring_role_arn = aws_iam_role.rds_monitoring.arn
  
  # Maintenance
  maintenance_window              = "sun:04:00-sun:05:00"
  apply_immediately              = false
  skip_final_snapshot            = var.environment != "production"
  final_snapshot_identifier_prefix = "microservices-final"
  
  # Subnet and security
  vpc_security_group_ids = [aws_security_group.rds.id]
  db_subnet_group_name   = module.vpc.database_subnet_group
  
  parameters = [
    {
      name  = "max_connections"
      value = "500"
    },
    {
      name  = "shared_buffers"
      value = "131072"  # 128MB * 1024 = 131072
    }
  ]
}

  engine                        = "redis"
  engine_version                = "7.0"
  node_type                     = var.environment == "production" ? "cache.r6g.large" : "cache.t4g.micro"
  num_cache_clusters            = var.environment == "production" ? 2 : 1
  automatic_failover_enabled    = var.environment == "production"
  multi_az_enabled              = var.environment == "production"
  
  port = 6379
  
  subnet_group_name = aws_elasticache_subnet_group.redis.name
  security_group_ids = [aws_security_group.redis.id]
  
  parameter_group_name = "default.redis7"
  
  at_rest_encryption_enabled  = true
  transit_encryption_enabled  = true
  auth_token                  = random_password.redis_auth.result
  
  backup_retention_limit      = var.environment == "production" ? 7 : 1
  backup_window               = "04:00-05:00"
  maintenance_window          = "sun:05:00-sun:06:00"
  
  tags = {
    Environment = var.environment
  }
}

# MSK (Managed Kafka)

  kafka_version = "3.4.0"

    instance_type = var.environment == "production" ? "kafka.m5.large" : "kafka.t3.small"
    client_subnets = module.vpc.private_subnets
    security_groups = [aws_security_group.msk.id]
    
    storage_info = {
      ebs_storage_info = {
        volume_size = var.environment == "production" ? 500 : 100
      }
    }
  }
  
  encryption_info = {
    encryption_in_transit = {
      client_broker = "TLS"
      in_cluster    = true
    }
  }
  
  client_authentication = {
    unauthenticated = false
    sasl = {
      scram = true
    }
  }
  
  configuration = {
    name        = "microservices-${var.environment}"
    description = "MSK configuration for microservices"
    
    server_properties = {
      "auto.create.topics.enable"                 = "false"
      "delete.topic.enable"                       = "true"
      "default.replication.factor"                = var.environment == "production" ? 3 : 2
      "min.insync.replicas"                       = "2"
      "unclean.leader.election.enable"            = "false"
      "log.retention.hours"                       = "168"
      "log.retention.bytes"                       = "107374182400"
      "compression.type"                          = "snappy"
    }
  }
}

# S3 for Artifacts and Static Assets

  force_destroy = var.environment != "production"

  }
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_versioning" "artifacts" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
  versioning_configuration {
    status = var.environment == "production" ? "Enabled" : "Suspended"
  }
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration" "artifacts" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
  
  rule {
    apply_server_side_encryption_by_default {
      sse_algorithm = "AES256"
    }
  }
}

resource "aws_s3_bucket_lifecycle_configuration" "artifacts" {
  bucket = aws_s3_bucket.artifacts.id
  
  rule {
    id     = "expire-old-artifacts"
    status = "Enabled"
    
    expiration {
      days = var.environment == "production" ? 90 : 30
    }
  }
}
